[0m$ [0mpython3 -c 'exec("""import json, urllib.request, datetime, collections, math, statistics
u=\"0x0bb81e516c88881c54529e1790470cb5e9f8740d\"
url=\"https://api.hyperliquid.xyz/info\"
h={\"content-type\":\"application/json\"}
def post(p):
 r=urllib.request.Request(url,data=json.dumps(p).encode(),headers=h,method=\"POST\")
 return json.loads(urllib.request.urlopen(r,timeout=60).read())
now=int(datetime.datetime.now(datetime.timezone.utc).timestamp()*1000)
fills=[]; start=0
while len(fills)<10000:
 x=post({\"type\":\"userFillsByTime\",\"user\":u,\"startTime\":start,\"endTime\":now,\"aggregateByTime\":True})
 if not x: break
 fills.extend(x)
 if len(x)<2000: break
 start=x[-1][\"time\"]-1
fills=sorted(fills,key=lambda f:f[\"time\"])
print(\"n\",len(fills),\"start\",datetime.datetime.fromtimestamp(fills[0][\"time\"]/1000,datetime.timezone.utc).isoformat(),\"end\",datetime.datetime.fromtimestamp(fills[-1][\"time\"]/1000,datetime.timezone.utc).isoformat())
# basic stats
for fld in [\"coin\",\"dir\",\"side\",\"crossed\"]:
 c=collections.Counter(f[fld] for f in fills)
 print(fld,c)
print(\"gross_notional\",sum(float(f[\"px\"])*float(f[\"sz\"]) for f in fills))
print(\"fees\",sum(float(f.get(\"fee\",0)) for f in fills))
print(\"closed_pnl\",sum(float(f.get(\"closedPnl\",0)) for f in fills))
# lots FIFO
lots=[]; closes=[]; current=0.0
for f in fills:
 sz=float(f[\"sz\"]); t=float(f[\"time\"]); px=float(f[\"px\"]); pnl=float(f.get(\"closedPnl\",0))
 if f[\"dir\"]==\"Open Long\":
  lots.append({\"sz\":sz,\"px\":px,\"t\":t})
  current+=sz
 elif f[\"dir\"]==\"Close Long\":
  rem=sz; consumed=[]
  while rem>1e-9 and lots:
   lot=lots[0]; take=min(rem,float(lot[\"sz\"])); consumed.append((take,lot)); rem-=take; lot[\"sz\"]-=take
   if lot[\"sz\"]<1e-9: lots.pop(0)
  if rem>1e-6: print(\"unmatched close\",f,rem)
  if consumed:
   entry=sum(a*b for a,b in consumed)/sz
   ret=(px-entry)/entry
   holds=[(t-b[\"t\"])/3600000 for a,b in consumed]
   closes.append({\"t\":t,\"px\":px,\"sz\":sz,\"entry\":entry,\"ret\":ret,\"hold_h\":sum(a*b for a,b in zip(consumed,holds))/sz,\"pnl\":pnl,\"fees\":float(f.get(\"fee\",0)),\"crossed\":f[\"crossed\"]})
print(\"closes\",len(closes),\"unmatched opens\",sum(x[\"sz\"] for x in lots),\"final pos\",current)
# metrics for closes, including partial lots as one close event
for key, vals in [(\"ret\",[c[\"ret\"] for c in closes]),(\"pnl\",[c[\"pnl\"] for c in closes]),(\"hold_h\",[c[\"hold_h\"] for c in closes]),(\"sz\",[c[\"sz\"] for c in closes])]:
 a=sorted(vals)
 def q(p): return a[min(len(a)-1,int(p*(len(a)-1)))] if a else None
 print(key,\"mean\",statistics.mean(vals) if vals else None,\"median\",statistics.median(vals) if vals else None,\"q10\",q(.1),\"q25\",q(.25),\"q75\",q(.75),\"q90\",q(.9),\"min\",min(vals) if vals else None,\"max\",max(vals) if vals else None)
win=[c for c in closes if c[\"pnl\"]>0]; lose=[c for c in closes if c[\"pnl\"]<0]; flat=[c for c in closes if c[\"pnl\"]==0]
print(\"winrate\",len(win)/len(closes) if closes else None,\"wins\",len(win),\"losses\",len(lose),\"flats\",len(flat))
print(\"grosswin\",sum(c[\"pnl\"] for c in win),\"grossloss\",sum(c[\"pnl\"] for c in lose),\"profit_factor\",sum(c[\"pnl\"] for c in win)/abs(sum(c[\"pnl\"] for c in lose)) if lose else None)
print(\"net_closed_pnl\",sum(c[\"pnl\"] for c in closes)-sum(c[\"fees\"] for c in closes))
# close return buckets
print(\"ret_buckets\",collections.Counter(\"<0\" if c[\"ret\"]<0 else \"0-0.5%\" if c[\"ret\"]<.005 else \"0.5-1%\" if c[\"ret\"]<.01 else \"1-2%\" if c[\"ret\"]<.02 else \">2%\" for c in closes))
# close size buckets
print(\"close_size_buckets\",collections.Counter(\"<1\" if c[\"sz\"]<1 else \"1-5\" if c[\"sz\"]<5 else \"5-10\" if c[\"sz\"]<10 else \"10-25\" if c[\"sz\"]<25 else \">25\" for c in closes))
# daily stats
byday=collections.defaultdict(lambda:{\"n\":0,\"pnl\":0.0,\"fees\":0.0,\"vol\":0.0,\"closes\":0})
for f in fills:
 d=datetime.datetime.fromtimestamp(f[\"time\"]/1000,datetime.timezone.utc).date().isoformat(); byday[d][\"n\"]+=1; byday[d][\"pnl\"]+=float(f.get(\"closedPnl\",0)); byday[d][\"fees\"]+=float(f.get(\"fee\",0)); byday[d][\"vol\"]+=float(f[\"px\"])*float(f[\"sz\"]); byday[d][\"closes\"]+=f[\"dir\"]==\"Close Long\"
print(\"days\",len(byday),\"active_days\",len(byday))
for d in sorted(byday): print(d,byday[d])
# open order size distributions from fills: entry sizes
opens=[f for f in fills if f[\"dir\"]==\"Open Long\"]
for fld in [\"sz\",\"px\"]:
 vals=sorted(float(f[fld]) for f in opens)
 print(\"opens\",fld,\"mean\",statistics.mean(vals),\"median\",statistics.median(vals),\"q10\",vals[int(.1*(len(vals)-1))],\"q90\",vals[int(.9*(len(vals)-1))],\"min\",min(vals),\"max\",max(vals))
# maker/taker by dir
for d in [\"Open Long\",\"Close Long\"]:
 fs=[f for f in fills if f[\"dir\"]==d]
 print(d,\"maker\",sum(not f[\"crossed\"] for f in fs),\"taker\",sum(f[\"crossed\"] for f in fs),\"fees\",sum(float(f.get(\"fee\",0)) for f in fs))

 return json.loads(urllib.request.urlopen(r,timeout=60).read())
now=int(datetime.datetime.now(datetime.timezone.utc).timestamp()*1000)
fills=[]; start=0
while len(fills)<10000:
 x=post({\"type\":\"userFillsByTime\",\"user\":u,\"startTime\":start,\"endTime\":now,\"aggregateByTime\":True})
 if not x: break
 fills.extend(x)
 if len(x)<2000: break
 start=x[-1][\"time\"]-1
fills=sorted(fills,key=lambda f:f[\"time\"])
print(\"n\",len(fills),\"start\",datetime.datetime.fromtimestamp(fills[0][\"time\"]/1000,datetime.timezone.utc).isoformat(),\"end\",datetime.datetime.fromtimestamp(fills[-1][\"time\"]/1000,datetime.timezone.utc).isoformat())

for fld in [\"coin\",\"dir\",\"side\",\"crossed\"]:
 c=collections.Counter(f[fld] for f in fills)
 print(fld,c)

print(\"fees\",round(sum(float(f.get(\"fee\",0)) for f in fills),4))
print(\"closed_pnl\",round(sum(float(f.get(\"closedPnl\",0)) for f in fills),4))
# FIFO lots; each close fill can consume multiple open lots
lots=[]; closes=[]; current=0.0
for f in fills:
 sz=float(f[\"sz\"]); t=float(f[\"time\"]); px=float(f[\"px\"]); pnl=float(f.get(\"closedPnl\",0))
 if f[\"dir\"]==\"Open Long\":
  lots.append({\"sz\":sz,\"px\":px,\"t\":t})
  current+=sz
 elif f[\"dir\"]==\"Close Long\":
  rem=sz; consumed=[]
  while rem>1e-9 and lots:
   lot=lots[0]; take=min(rem,float(lot[\"sz\"])); consumed.append((take,lot)); rem-=take; lot[\"sz\"]-=take
   if lot[\"sz\"]<1e-9: lots.pop(0)
  if rem>1e-6: print(\"unmatched close\",f,rem)
  if consumed:
   entry=sum(a*b for a,b in consumed)/sz
   holds=[(t-b[\"t\"])/3600000 for a,b in consumed]
   closes.append({\"t\":t,\"px\":px,\"sz\":sz,\"entry\":entry,\"ret\":(px-entry)/entry,\"hold_h\":sum(a*b for a,b in zip(consumed,holds))/sz,\"pnl\":pnl,\"fees\":float(f.get(\"fee\",0)),\"crossed\":f[\"crossed\"]})
print(\"closes\",len(closes),\"unmatched opens\",sum(x[\"sz\"] for x in lots),\"final pos\",current)
for key, vals in [(\"ret\",[c[\"ret\"] for c in closes]),(\"pnl\",[c[\"pnl\"] for c in closes]),(\"hold_h\",[c[\"hold_h\"] for c in closes]),(\"sz\",[c[\"sz\"] for c in closes])]:
 a=sorted(vals)
 def q(p): return a[min(len(a)-1,int(p*(len(a)-1)))] if a else None
 print(key,\"mean\",round(statistics.mean(vals),6) if vals else None,\"median\",round(statistics.median(vals),6) if vals else None,\"q10\",round(q(.1),6),\"q25\",round(q(.25),6),\"q75\",round(q(.75),6),\"q90\",round(q(.9),6),\"min\",round(min(vals),6) if vals else None,\"max\",round(max(vals),6) if vals else None)
win=[c for c in closes if c[\"pnl\"]>0]; lose=[c for c in closes if c[\"pnl\"]<0]; flat=[c for c in closes if c[\"pnl\"]==0]
print(\"winrate\",len(win)/len(closes) if closes else None,\"wins\",len(win),\"losses\",len(lose),\"flats\",len(flat))
print(\"grosswin\",round(sum(c[\"pnl\"] for c in win),4),\"grossloss\",round(sum(c[\"pnl\"] for c in lose),4),\"profit_factor\",round(sum(c[\"pnl\"] for c in win)/abs(sum(c[\"pnl\"] for c in lose)),4) if lose else None)
print(\"net_closed_pnl_after_all_fills\",round(sum(c[\"pnl\"] for c in closes)-sum(c[\"fees\"] for c in fills),4))
print(\"ret_buckets\",collections.Counter(\"<0\" if c[\"ret\"]<0 else \"0-0.5%\" if c[\"ret\"]<.005 else \"0.5-1%\" if c[\"ret\"]<.01 else \"1-2%\" if c[\"ret\"]<.02 else \">2%\" for c in closes))
print(\"close_size_buckets\",collections.Counter(\"<1\" if c[\"sz\"]<1 else \"1-5\" if c[\"sz\"]<5 else \"5-10\" if c[\"sz\"]<10 else \"10-25\" if c[\"sz\"]<25 else \">25\" for c in closes))
